Findings in full

CRITICAL beh.executed_ratio — WebAttack-XSS: 96.0% of flows never sent a byte (652 of 679)

WebAttack-XSS cannot execute without the attacker sending payload, so these flows are connection attempts labelled as the attack itself. They inflate web_attack and, because they are trivially separable from real attack traffic, they inflate any accuracy figure measured on it.

SELECT label_executed, count(*) AS flows
FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/labelled/cic-ids-2017/**/*.parquet', union_by_name=true)
WHERE label_raw = 'WebAttack-XSS'
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC
CRITICAL int.bytes_physically_possible — 813 flow(s) report more payload bytes than wire bytes across 3 class(es); dos materially affected

Payload bytes are a subset of the bytes that crossed the wire, so a flow where orig_bytes exceeds orig_ip_bytes (or resp_bytes exceeds resp_ip_bytes) is arithmetically impossible: Zeek derives the first from TCP sequence numbers, which wrap on scan and RST traffic, and counts the second off the wire, which cannot. These are the same columns that carry the largest label correlation in 2017 (point-biserial -0.442) and the largest attack-class mutual information (0.835), so the corruption sits exactly where the label signal is and every expectation or model built on it inherits the wrap. Use orig_ip_bytes and resp_ip_bytes instead.

CRITICAL orc.missed_attacks — 3,475 benign flow(s) are attacks according to the external oracle, 6,276 of them in 3 window(s) CIC is known to have got wrong

These flows carry no rule match, so they are benign only because the published schedule does not mention them -- and an independent manual re-labelling of the same packets says an attack was running. Every one is currently training data for the benign class. 6,276 of them fall inside a window the CNS2022 forensics already established that CIC mislabelled (Botnet Ares failed-C2 tail, Infiltration Cool Disk - MAC, Infiltration NMAP scan, early start), where the fault is ours by inheritance: the transcription is faithful and the source schedule is wrong. The correction belongs in the manifest. A further 5,095 flow(s) match only `- Attempted` oracle rows and are excluded from the count above: an attempt that delivered nothing is a defensible thing to call benign.

    conn AS (SELECT * FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/labelled/cic-ids-2017/**/*.parquet', union_by_name = true))
    SELECT r.rid,
           CAST(floor((c.ts - r.t0) / 10) AS BIGINT) AS b,
           count(*) AS n
    FROM conn c JOIN rules r
      ON c.ts >= r.t0 - 600 AND c.ts < r.t1 + 600
     AND (
    (r.atk IS NOT NULL AND (
        (list_contains(r.atk, c."id.orig_h") AND (
        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.resp_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
     OR (list_contains(r.atk, c."id.resp_h") AND (
        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
    ))
    OR (r.atk IS NULL AND r.vic IS NOT NULL
        AND (list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h") OR list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h")))
  )
    GROUP BY 1, 2
CRITICAL sch.window_sweep — DoS-Slowhttptest 2017-07-05 10:14-10:35: no traffic edge at window end

The rate inside this window is 12.1 flows/s against 1.6 before and 43.4 after. A correct window separates two regimes; this one does not at its end, so the schedule boundary is not where the traffic changes. The rate actually rises +1810s from the declared start. It falls +1800s from the declared start.

CRITICAL xds.benign_baseline — cic-ddos-2019's benign class is closer to its own 'ddos' (JS 0.106) than to cic-ids-2018's benign (JS 0.450)

Benign traffic from two testbeds should resemble each other more than either resembles an attack. cic-ddos-2019's does not, which means its benign class is largely unlabelled attack traffic. It cannot serve as a false-positive baseline, and any accuracy computed against it is measuring contamination.

CRITICAL xds.class_coheres — 'brute_force' differs more between cic-ids-2017 and cic-ids-2018 (JS 0.539) than 'ddos' and 'dos' differ inside one dataset (JS 0.153)

A canonical class should look more like itself in another testbed than like a different attack in the same testbed. 'brute_force' does not: it spans 3.5x the distance that separates two genuinely different classes. Whatever a cross-domain result about 'brute_force' measures, it is not one phenomenon.

FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/labelled/cic-ids-2017/**/*.parquet', union_by_name=true)
WHERE label_class = 'dos' AND label_raw = 'DoS-Slowhttptest'

WITH rules AS (SELECT * FROM (VALUES
        (7, 'WebAttack-BruteForce', 'web_attack', 1499343600.0, 1499346060.0, ['205.174.165.73', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.10.50', '205.174.165.68'], CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR)),
        (8, 'WebAttack-XSS', 'web_attack', 1499346900.0, 1499348160.0, ['205.174.165.73', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.10.50', '205.174.165.68'], CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR)),
        (9, 'WebAttack-SQLInjection', 'web_attack', 1499348400.0, 1499348580.0, ['205.174.165.73', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.10.50', '205.174.165.68'], CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR))
      ) AS t(rid, rname, rclass, t0, t1, eps, vpfx)),
      ev AS (SELECT "ts", "uid", "id.orig_h", "id.resp_h", "uri", "method", "status_code" FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/parquets/cic-ids-2017/**/http.parquet', union_by_name=true)
        WHERE ts IS NOT NULL AND ts >= 1499343600.0 AND ts < 1499348580.0)
      SELECT r.rid, r.rname, count(*) AS records,
             count(DISTINCT e."uid") AS uids,
             sum(CASE WHEN regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:union[^a-z0-9]{1,12}select)|(?:select[^;]{1,80}from)|(?:(?:''|%27)[^a-z0-9]{0,8}(?:or|and)[^a-z0-9]{1,8}(?:''|%27)?[0-9]{1,3}[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=)|(?:\bor[^a-z0-9]{1,4}1[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=[^a-z0-9]{0,4}1)|(?:information_schema|table_schema)|(?:(?:sleep|benchmark|waitfor[^a-z]{1,6}delay)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:group_concat|concat_ws|xp_cmdshell|load_file)') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS sqli,
             sum(CASE WHEN regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:<script|%3cscript|&lt;script)|(?:</script|%3c%2fscript)|(?:javascript[:%])|(?:on(?:error|load|mouseover|focus|click)[^a-z]{0,6}=)|(?:(?:alert|prompt|confirm)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:document[^a-z]{0,4}\.[^a-z]{0,4}cookie)|(?:<(?:img|svg|iframe|body)[^a-z]|%3c(?:img|svg|iframe|body))') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS xss,
             count(DISTINCT CASE WHEN regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:union[^a-z0-9]{1,12}select)|(?:select[^;]{1,80}from)|(?:(?:''|%27)[^a-z0-9]{0,8}(?:or|and)[^a-z0-9]{1,8}(?:''|%27)?[0-9]{1,3}[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=)|(?:\bor[^a-z0-9]{1,4}1[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=[^a-z0-9]{0,4}1)|(?:information_schema|table_schema)|(?:(?:sleep|benchmark|waitfor[^a-z]{1,6}delay)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:group_concat|concat_ws|xp_cmdshell|load_file)') OR regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:<script|%3cscript|&lt;script)|(?:</script|%3c%2fscript)|(?:javascript[:%])|(?:on(?:error|load|mouseover|focus|click)[^a-z]{0,6}=)|(?:(?:alert|prompt|confirm)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:document[^a-z]{0,4}\.[^a-z]{0,4}cookie)|(?:<(?:img|svg|iframe|body)[^a-z]|%3c(?:img|svg|iframe|body))') THEN e."uid" END) AS uids_sig,
             sum(CASE WHEN upper(coalesce(e."method", '')) = 'GET' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS gets,
             sum(CASE WHEN upper(coalesce(e."method", '')) = 'POST' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS posts,
             sum(CASE WHEN (regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:union[^a-z0-9]{1,12}select)|(?:select[^;]{1,80}from)|(?:(?:''|%27)[^a-z0-9]{0,8}(?:or|and)[^a-z0-9]{1,8}(?:''|%27)?[0-9]{1,3}[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=)|(?:\bor[^a-z0-9]{1,4}1[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=[^a-z0-9]{0,4}1)|(?:information_schema|table_schema)|(?:(?:sleep|benchmark|waitfor[^a-z]{1,6}delay)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:group_concat|concat_ws|xp_cmdshell|load_file)') OR regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:<script|%3cscript|&lt;script)|(?:</script|%3c%2fscript)|(?:javascript[:%])|(?:on(?:error|load|mouseover|focus|click)[^a-z]{0,6}=)|(?:(?:alert|prompt|confirm)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:document[^a-z]{0,4}\.[^a-z]{0,4}cookie)|(?:<(?:img|svg|iframe|body)[^a-z]|%3c(?:img|svg|iframe|body))')) AND e."status_code" BETWEEN 200 AND 299
                      THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS sig_2xx
      FROM rules r JOIN ev e ON e.ts >= r.t0 AND e.ts < r.t1
          AND (coalesce(list_contains(r.eps, e."id.orig_h"), false)
            OR coalesce(list_contains(r.eps, e."id.resp_h"), false)
            OR coalesce(starts_with(e."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
            OR coalesce(starts_with(e."id.resp_h", r.vpfx), false)
            OR (r.eps IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL))
      GROUP BY r.rid, r.rname ORDER BY r.rid
MAJOR int.null_not_zero — duration is null for 8.10% of dos flows

37,090 flow(s) carry no duration. The EDA spec coalesces this feature's nulls to 0, so those flows are indistinguishable from flows that genuinely measured zero, and a class whose zero mass is really missing data will look flood-shaped whether or not it is. Nothing here says the labels are wrong -- it constrains which features a downstream claim about the affected classes may rest on, and requires that nulls be excluded explicitly rather than read as zeros.

     OR (list_contains(r.atk, c."id.resp_h") AND (
        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
    ))
    OR (r.atk IS NULL AND r.vic IS NOT NULL
        AND (list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h") OR list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h")))
  )
    GROUP BY 1, 2

  ) AS endpoint_match
      FROM conn c JOIN rules r ON c.ts >= r.t0 AND c.ts < r.t1)
    SELECT rid, rname,
           count(*) AS in_window,
           sum(CASE WHEN endpoint_match THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS matched
    FROM inwin GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY in_window DESC

        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
    ))
    OR (r.atk IS NULL AND r.vic IS NOT NULL
        AND (list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h") OR list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h")))
  ) AS endpoint_match
      FROM conn c JOIN rules r ON c.ts >= r.t0 AND c.ts < r.t1)
    SELECT rid, rname,
           count(*) AS in_window,
           sum(CASE WHEN endpoint_match THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS matched
    FROM inwin GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY in_window DESC

             (
    (r.atk IS NOT NULL AND (
        (list_contains(r.atk, c."id.orig_h") AND (
        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.resp_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))

        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
    ))
    OR (r.atk IS NULL AND r.vic IS NOT NULL
        AND (list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h") OR list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h")))
  ) AS endpoint_match
      FROM conn c JOIN rules r ON c.ts >= r.t0 AND c.ts < r.t1)
    SELECT rid, rname,
           count(*) AS in_window,
           sum(CASE WHEN endpoint_match THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS matched
    FROM inwin GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY in_window DESC

        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)

        AND (list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h") OR list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h")))
  ) AS endpoint_match
      FROM conn c JOIN rules r ON c.ts >= r.t0 AND c.ts < r.t1)
    SELECT rid, rname,
           count(*) AS in_window,
           sum(CASE WHEN endpoint_match THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS matched
    FROM inwin GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY in_window DESC

    conn AS (SELECT * FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/labelled/cic-ids-2017/**/*.parquet', union_by_name = true))
    SELECT r.rid,
           CAST(floor((c.ts - r.t0) / 10) AS BIGINT) AS b,
           count(*) AS n
    FROM conn c JOIN rules r

     AND (
    (r.atk IS NOT NULL AND (
        (list_contains(r.atk, c."id.orig_h") AND (
        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.resp_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
     OR (list_contains(r.atk, c."id.resp_h") AND (
        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
    ))
    OR (r.atk IS NULL AND r.vic IS NOT NULL
        AND (list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h") OR list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h")))
  )
    GROUP BY 1, 2

MAJOR sch.rule_ambiguity — 1 rule pair(s) overlap in time and endpoints, 1 across different classes

Where two rules can both claim a flow, the lower rule id wins. That is manifest ordering, not evidence. Overlaps spanning two different canonical classes are the ones that matter: which class a flow lands in is then decided by YAML order.

Metrics

metricvalue
overlapping_pairs1
cross_class_pairs1

        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.resp_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
     OR (list_contains(r.atk, c."id.resp_h") AND (
        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h"), false)
        OR coalesce(starts_with(c."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
      ))
    ))
    OR (r.atk IS NULL AND r.vic IS NOT NULL
        AND (list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h") OR list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h")))
  )
    GROUP BY 1, 2

  ) AS r(rid, rname, rclass, t0, t1, atk, vic, vpfx, needs_payload)),
    conn AS (SELECT * FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/labelled/cic-ids-2017/**/*.parquet', union_by_name = true))
    SELECT r.rid,
           CAST(floor((c.ts - r.t0) / 10) AS BIGINT) AS b,
           count(*) AS n

      ON c.ts >= r.t0 - 600 AND c.ts < r.t1 + 600
     AND (

        (list_contains(r.atk, c."id.orig_h") AND (
        (r.vic IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL)
        OR coalesce(list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h"), false)

    OR (r.atk IS NULL AND r.vic IS NOT NULL
        AND (list_contains(r.vic, c."id.orig_h") OR list_contains(r.vic, c."id.resp_h")))
  )
    GROUP BY 1, 2
MAJOR xds.class_coheres — 'botnet' differs more between cic-ids-2017 and cic-ids-2018 (JS 0.300) than 'ddos' and 'dos' differ inside one dataset (JS 0.153)

A canonical class should look more like itself in another testbed than like a different attack in the same testbed. 'botnet' does not: it spans 2.0x the distance that separates two genuinely different classes. Whatever a cross-domain result about 'botnet' measures, it is not one phenomenon.

MINOR con.duplicate_conflict — 348 flow(s) are provably mislabelled (0.0164%): identical flows carrying different classes

285 distinct feature vectors appear in the labelled zone under more than one class. The vectors are built from every observable conn column with identity and timing removed, so the flows in a group are indistinguishable to any model. Taking the majority label of each group as correct, 348 flows are labelled wrongly -- a lower bound, since a whole group could be wrong together. Empty and near-empty flows dominate these groups in practice, which is the signature of a time-window label catching connection attempts that carry no attack.

WITH vec AS (
    SELECT "id.orig_p" AS k0, "id.resp_p" AS k1, "proto" AS k2, "service" AS k3, "duration" AS k4, "orig_bytes" AS k5, "resp_bytes" AS k6, "conn_state" AS k7, "local_orig" AS k8, "local_resp" AS k9, "missed_bytes" AS k10, "history" AS k11, "orig_pkts" AS k12, "orig_ip_bytes" AS k13, "resp_pkts" AS k14, "resp_ip_bytes" AS k15, "ip_proto" AS k16, label_class, count(*) AS n
    FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/labelled/cic-ids-2017/**/*.parquet', union_by_name=true)
    GROUP BY ALL
), grp AS (
    SELECT *,
           sum(n)   OVER w AS grp_flows,
           count(*) OVER w AS grp_classes,
           row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY k0, k1, k2, k3, k4, k5, k6, k7, k8, k9, k10, k11, k12, k13, k14, k15, k16
                              ORDER BY n DESC, label_class) AS rk
    FROM vec WINDOW w AS (PARTITION BY k0, k1, k2, k3, k4, k5, k6, k7, k8, k9, k10, k11, k12, k13, k14, k15, k16)
)
SELECT * FROM grp WHERE grp_classes > 1
MINOR int.null_not_zero — service is null for 100.00% of infiltration flows and 4 other class(es)

344,626 flow(s) carry no service. Nulls in this feature stay visible as their own level rather than collapsing onto a real value, but they are still absent evidence rather than a measurement. Nothing here says the labels are wrong -- it constrains which features a downstream claim about the affected classes may rest on, and requires that nulls be excluded explicitly rather than read as zeros.

MINOR orc.attempted_axis — 18,491 of 532,310 matched attack flow(s) (3.47%) are `- Attempted` in the external oracle

The oracle records, per flow, whether an attack actually executed and why not. Our labels have no such axis, so this is a gap in our label model rather than an error in it -- nothing here says a label is wrong, and no disagreement involving this axis is counted against us anywhere in this tier. What it constrains is what an attack class may be quoted as evidence OF: a class whose matched flows are largely attempts is a class of connection attempts, and a flow count taken from it is not a count of executed attacks. 2 class(es) are above 25%.

MINOR sch.interval_overlap_delta — 21 flow(s) (0.001%) change label when matched by interval overlap instead of start time

A flow is currently an attack only if it STARTED inside the window. Matching on overlap instead -- the flow was alive at some point during the attack -- moves these flows. Slow-DoS attacks hold connections open by design, so for those the start-only rule systematically under-labels.

      GROUP BY c.uid)
    SELECT c.label_class, r.rclass AS overlap_class,
           count(*) AS n, sum(coalesce(c.duration, 0)) AS total_duration
    FROM conn c LEFT JOIN ov USING (uid)
    LEFT JOIN rules r ON r.rid = ov.rid
    WHERE coalesce(r.rclass, 'benign') <> c.label_class
    GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY n DESC

INFO art.cicflowmeter_artefacts — CICFlowMeter artefact probes ran over 2,119,182 flows and found none of the signatures

Reported because the negative result is the finding. Rosay et al. (ICISSP 2022) identified four artefact classes that CICFlowMeter writes into every CSV it generates -- constant bulk features from an integer division, ARP frames read as IPv4 traffic between 8.0.6.4 and 8.6.0.1, non-TCP/UDP packets collapsed to protocol 0 with ports 0, and TCP flows cut at the first FIN whose halves can end up labelled differently -- and stated that every dataset shipping CICFlowMeter CSVs carries them, CIC-IDS-2017, CSE-CIC-IDS-2018 and CIC-DDoS-2019 included. Talos does not use those CSVs; the lake is Zeek output over the original pcaps. This check is the measurement behind that claim, so that 'our features do not carry the published artefacts' is a reading rather than an assertion. Probe 1 has no literal equivalent in Zeek's schema and is reported as inapplicable rather than faked.

SELECT count(*), sum(CASE WHEN "id.orig_h" IN ('8.0.6.4', '8.6.0.1') OR "id.resp_h" IN ('8.0.6.4', '8.6.0.1') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), sum(CASE WHEN ((proto IS NULL OR lower(CAST(proto AS VARCHAR)) IN ('0', '', 'unknown', 'none')) AND coalesce("id.orig_p", 0) = 0 AND coalesce("id.resp_p", 0) = 0 AND NOT ("id.orig_h" IN ('8.0.6.4', '8.6.0.1') OR "id.resp_h" IN ('8.0.6.4', '8.6.0.1'))) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), sum(CASE WHEN proto = 'unknown_transport' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), sum(CASE WHEN (proto = 'tcp' AND history IS NOT NULL AND contains(history, 'F') AND contains(history, 'f')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), min("duration"), max("duration"), count("duration"), min("orig_bytes"), max("orig_bytes"), count("orig_bytes"), min("resp_bytes"), max("resp_bytes"), count("resp_bytes"), min("missed_bytes"), max("missed_bytes"), count("missed_bytes"), min("orig_pkts"), max("orig_pkts"), count("orig_pkts"), min("orig_ip_bytes"), max("orig_ip_bytes"), count("orig_ip_bytes"), min("resp_pkts"), max("resp_pkts"), count("resp_pkts"), min("resp_ip_bytes"), max("resp_ip_bytes"), count("resp_ip_bytes")
FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/labelled/cic-ids-2017/**/*.parquet', union_by_name = true);
SELECT count(*) AS groups, coalesce(sum(n), 0) AS flows FROM (
            SELECT "id.orig_h", "id.orig_p", "id.resp_h", "id.resp_p", ts, count(*) AS n
            FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/labelled/cic-ids-2017/**/*.parquet', union_by_name = true)
            WHERE (proto = 'tcp' AND history IS NOT NULL AND contains(history, 'F') AND contains(history, 'f'))
            GROUP BY "id.orig_h", "id.orig_p", "id.resp_h", "id.resp_p", ts
            HAVING count(DISTINCT label_binary) > 1)

WITH rules AS (SELECT * FROM (VALUES
        (1, 'SSH-Patator', 'brute_force', 1499187600.0, 1499191260.0, ['205.174.165.73', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.10.50', '205.174.165.68'], CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR))
      ) AS t(rid, rname, rclass, t0, t1, eps, vpfx)),
          ev AS (SELECT "ts", "uid", "id.orig_h", "id.resp_h", "auth_attempts", "auth_success", "client" FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/parquets/cic-ids-2017/**/ssh.parquet', union_by_name=true)
        WHERE ts IS NOT NULL AND ts >= 1499187600.0 AND ts < 1499191260.0)
          SELECT r.rid, count(*) AS records,
                 count(DISTINCT e."uid") AS uids,
                 sum(coalesce(e."auth_attempts", 0)) AS attempts,
                 max(e."auth_attempts") AS max_attempts, sum(CASE WHEN e."auth_success" THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS successes,
                 count(DISTINCT e."id.orig_h") AS sources
          FROM rules r JOIN ev e ON e.ts >= r.t0 AND e.ts < r.t1
          AND (coalesce(list_contains(r.eps, e."id.orig_h"), false)
            OR coalesce(list_contains(r.eps, e."id.resp_h"), false)
            OR coalesce(starts_with(e."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
            OR coalesce(starts_with(e."id.resp_h", r.vpfx), false)
            OR (r.eps IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL))
          GROUP BY r.rid ORDER BY r.rid

WITH rules AS (SELECT * FROM (VALUES
        (0, 'FTP-Patator', 'brute_force', 1499170800.0, 1499174460.0, ['205.174.165.73', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.10.50', '205.174.165.68'], CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR))
      ) AS t(rid, rname, rclass, t0, t1, eps, vpfx)),
          ev AS (SELECT "ts", "uid", "id.orig_h", "id.resp_h", "user", "password", "command", "reply_code" FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/parquets/cic-ids-2017/**/ftp.parquet', union_by_name=true)
        WHERE ts IS NOT NULL AND ts >= 1499170800.0 AND ts < 1499174460.0)
          SELECT r.rid, count(*) AS records,
                 count(DISTINCT e."uid") AS uids,
                 count(DISTINCT e."user") AS users,
                 sum(CASE WHEN e."reply_code" = 530 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failures,
                 sum(CASE WHEN e."reply_code" = 230 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS successes,
                 count(DISTINCT e."id.orig_h") AS sources
          FROM rules r JOIN ev e ON e.ts >= r.t0 AND e.ts < r.t1
          AND (coalesce(list_contains(r.eps, e."id.orig_h"), false)
            OR coalesce(list_contains(r.eps, e."id.resp_h"), false)
            OR coalesce(starts_with(e."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
            OR coalesce(starts_with(e."id.resp_h", r.vpfx), false)
            OR (r.eps IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL))
          GROUP BY r.rid ORDER BY r.rid

WITH rules AS (SELECT * FROM (VALUES
        (7, 'WebAttack-BruteForce', 'web_attack', 1499343600.0, 1499346060.0, ['205.174.165.73', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.10.50', '205.174.165.68'], CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR)),
        (8, 'WebAttack-XSS', 'web_attack', 1499346900.0, 1499348160.0, ['205.174.165.73', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.10.50', '205.174.165.68'], CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR)),
        (9, 'WebAttack-SQLInjection', 'web_attack', 1499348400.0, 1499348580.0, ['205.174.165.73', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.10.50', '205.174.165.68'], CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR))
      ) AS t(rid, rname, rclass, t0, t1, eps, vpfx)),
      ev AS (SELECT "ts", "uid", "id.orig_h", "id.resp_h", "uri", "method", "status_code" FROM read_parquet('s3://ids-datalakec48eb2cab942494ba5059fac3b3527d9/parquets/cic-ids-2017/**/http.parquet', union_by_name=true)
        WHERE ts IS NOT NULL AND ts >= 1499343600.0 AND ts < 1499348580.0)
      SELECT r.rid, r.rname, count(*) AS records,
             count(DISTINCT e."uid") AS uids,
             sum(CASE WHEN regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:union[^a-z0-9]{1,12}select)|(?:select[^;]{1,80}from)|(?:(?:''|%27)[^a-z0-9]{0,8}(?:or|and)[^a-z0-9]{1,8}(?:''|%27)?[0-9]{1,3}[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=)|(?:\bor[^a-z0-9]{1,4}1[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=[^a-z0-9]{0,4}1)|(?:information_schema|table_schema)|(?:(?:sleep|benchmark|waitfor[^a-z]{1,6}delay)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:group_concat|concat_ws|xp_cmdshell|load_file)') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS sqli,
             sum(CASE WHEN regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:<script|%3cscript|&lt;script)|(?:</script|%3c%2fscript)|(?:javascript[:%])|(?:on(?:error|load|mouseover|focus|click)[^a-z]{0,6}=)|(?:(?:alert|prompt|confirm)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:document[^a-z]{0,4}\.[^a-z]{0,4}cookie)|(?:<(?:img|svg|iframe|body)[^a-z]|%3c(?:img|svg|iframe|body))') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS xss,
             count(DISTINCT CASE WHEN regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:union[^a-z0-9]{1,12}select)|(?:select[^;]{1,80}from)|(?:(?:''|%27)[^a-z0-9]{0,8}(?:or|and)[^a-z0-9]{1,8}(?:''|%27)?[0-9]{1,3}[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=)|(?:\bor[^a-z0-9]{1,4}1[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=[^a-z0-9]{0,4}1)|(?:information_schema|table_schema)|(?:(?:sleep|benchmark|waitfor[^a-z]{1,6}delay)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:group_concat|concat_ws|xp_cmdshell|load_file)') OR regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:<script|%3cscript|&lt;script)|(?:</script|%3c%2fscript)|(?:javascript[:%])|(?:on(?:error|load|mouseover|focus|click)[^a-z]{0,6}=)|(?:(?:alert|prompt|confirm)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:document[^a-z]{0,4}\.[^a-z]{0,4}cookie)|(?:<(?:img|svg|iframe|body)[^a-z]|%3c(?:img|svg|iframe|body))') THEN e."uid" END) AS uids_sig,
             sum(CASE WHEN upper(coalesce(e."method", '')) = 'GET' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS gets,
             sum(CASE WHEN upper(coalesce(e."method", '')) = 'POST' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS posts,
             sum(CASE WHEN (regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:union[^a-z0-9]{1,12}select)|(?:select[^;]{1,80}from)|(?:(?:''|%27)[^a-z0-9]{0,8}(?:or|and)[^a-z0-9]{1,8}(?:''|%27)?[0-9]{1,3}[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=)|(?:\bor[^a-z0-9]{1,4}1[^a-z0-9]{0,4}=[^a-z0-9]{0,4}1)|(?:information_schema|table_schema)|(?:(?:sleep|benchmark|waitfor[^a-z]{1,6}delay)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:group_concat|concat_ws|xp_cmdshell|load_file)') OR regexp_matches(lower(coalesce(e."uri", '')), '(?:<script|%3cscript|&lt;script)|(?:</script|%3c%2fscript)|(?:javascript[:%])|(?:on(?:error|load|mouseover|focus|click)[^a-z]{0,6}=)|(?:(?:alert|prompt|confirm)[^a-z]{0,4}\()|(?:document[^a-z]{0,4}\.[^a-z]{0,4}cookie)|(?:<(?:img|svg|iframe|body)[^a-z]|%3c(?:img|svg|iframe|body))')) AND e."status_code" BETWEEN 200 AND 299
                      THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS sig_2xx
      FROM rules r JOIN ev e ON e.ts >= r.t0 AND e.ts < r.t1
          AND (coalesce(list_contains(r.eps, e."id.orig_h"), false)
            OR coalesce(list_contains(r.eps, e."id.resp_h"), false)
            OR coalesce(starts_with(e."id.orig_h", r.vpfx), false)
            OR coalesce(starts_with(e."id.resp_h", r.vpfx), false)
            OR (r.eps IS NULL AND r.vpfx IS NULL))
      GROUP BY r.rid, r.rname ORDER BY r.rid
INFO orc.class_agreement — external agreement 99.34% over 2,107,787 direction-agreeing matches

Per canonical class, the share of time-overlapping matches on which an independent manual re-labelling assigns the same class we do. Restricted to matches where both sides agree which end originated the connection, so the comparison is of two readings of the same traffic rather than of two flow-construction conventions. Oracle labels with no mapping into our taxonomy are excluded from the denominator, never counted as agreement.

INFO orc.coverage — external oracle matches 99.76% of our flows (2,114,093 of 2,119,182)

Share of our flows for which some manually-forensicated oracle row overlaps in time on the same unordered 5-tuple. This number gates every other figure in this tier: an agreement rate computed over a small unrepresentative match set describes the match set, not the labels. Flow COUNTS are deliberately not compared -- CICFlowMeter's 120-second active timeout splits one Zeek connection into many oracle rows, which is what the fan-out columns show.

INFO orc.direction_reversal — 6,306 of 2,114,093 oracle matches (0.30%) have the originator reversed

CICFlowMeter fixes direction from the first packet it sees and Zeek from the SYN, so the two disagree wherever a handshake is absent. The join keys on the unordered 5-tuple precisely so these flows are matched instead of lost, and this is the measurement that would otherwise be invisible. It indicts neither labelling. It does bound the rest of the tier -- agreement is computed over direction-agreeing matches only -- and it means any orig/resp-relative feature is not describing the same endpoint on both sides.

INFO orc.false_attacks — 5,385 attack flow(s) are benign according to the external oracle (1.02% of matched attack flows)

Our labels are a time window and a pair of addresses, so a window wider than the attack collects whatever else those hosts were doing. These flows are in an attack class and the independent re-labelling reads them as ordinary traffic. They inflate the class and credit any detector evaluated on them with finding something that was not there.
